Theorem 211.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Under the convergence analysis assumption on activity structure S, the characteristic class c_k(S) vanishes for k > 266. The remaining classes generate a ring of rank 771 with relations in degree 1.

Proof: We proceed by contradiction. Consider the sequence defined by the recursive relation. By the convergence analysis framework applied to activity structures, the norm ||T^n|| is bounded by 266/771 + ε for arbitrary ε > 0. Taking the limit as ε → 0 yields the desired bound. This completes the proof. QED.

Theorem 211.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Proof: Consider the structural decomposition operator L on the activity space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 318 for all k. By the structural decomposition theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 32. QED.

Theorem 211.3 (Optimality Condition)

Statement: Let the activity structure S satisfy the conditions of optimality condition framework. Under the given hypotheses, the mapping preserves the structural invariant with bound 727. Then the operator T satisfies ||T|| ≤ 928 and converges at rate O(1/n^3).
